===== english/316/report.md =====
# C05:316 — independent English-first proposal
Producer: Codex. All alignments remain PROVISIONAL; no hgm_gloss changes or semantic approval.
Read the full original source and C05:310–324 context. Original spine fields, full material corpus/master objects, physical bytes and historical governing inputs are retained under ../originals and ../evidence.
Challenge the split zad/end and byed pa/brings first: their causative reading is source-specific, and supplied to an stays unwrapped. The final yin/is belongs to the fourth line, while represent is left unbanked.
Every retained span, selected occurrence, member parent, and every unwrapped source/English range is recorded in decisions.json. Uncertain/distributed correspondences are omitted with reasons, not converted to nulls.
Full five-ground screens are in five-ground-errata-review.json; errata.json is empty. Corpus equality proves only the compared text; no independent physical publication was inspected.
Canonical generator: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=3450 bytes. The actual complete stdout is body.html and native/generate-v1/stdout.bin; command, stdin, execution time and exit are preserved beside it.
Canonical resolver: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; all intended source and English ranges match. Complete native output is native/resolve-v1/stdout.bin. Successful generation was run once for this unchanged spec.
Measured: 20 spans; 0 nulls; 15 non-null d5 word pairs; 15 total d5; 2 d7 members.

Limits: no publication repair, repository acceptance, device integration, or production fidelity suite was performed by this original-proposal role. Independent reconciliation and fresh review are still separate requirements.

===== reconciled/316/report.md =====
C05:316 — independent Codex reconciliation; every alignment remains PROVISIONAL.
The ending-agent nominal phrase is decomposed into de/it, zad/end, and byed pa/brings using the complete local causative and exact HGM support. Its former full d3 alternative is preserved with a recast disposition. Retain the positive virtue and two unstained members; no Non-affix proposal.
22 spans; 1 null; 15 non-null d5 word pairs; 15 total d5; 3 d7 members; 0 retained errata.
The topic null asserts only no separately alignable English word; subject-predicate syntax preserves its function. Uncertainty and distributed translation are omitted with reasons.
All 38 original proposals for this segment have exact identity/range/parent-aware dispositions in decisions.json; all final tuples and complete unwrapped ranges are recorded there.
Five refutation grounds for each suspicion are in five-ground-errata-review.json, with full original source, physical, corpus, master and registered-class evidence preserved through ../original-to-copy-manifest.json.
Original source/governance origin is 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309. No later through315 acceptance baseline was consumed.
Original generators: both angle replays EXIT=0, empty stderr, exact saved bodies; original resolver replays EXIT=0. All historical/superseded originals remain preserved.
Final generator EXIT=0; stdout=body.html, 4052 bytes; stderr=0 bytes. Actual native/generate-v1 contains stdin, stdout, stderr, exit and execution metadata.
Final resolver EXIT=0; all intended ranges match; rendered Tibetan and English exactly reproduce original source fields after removing generated null markers.
Spec 4087 bytes, SHA256 ff328b658bed8e85fd4c9430982875490bdaf8cfd110b8da4fa6e0e5947e5fa2; body SHA256 04e5e2800f34f04cded3dc8aa18e52d94748fd44b28cd51c40c8b5246e4aaa9d.
No supplied-head allowance is requested. Canonical generator, historical builder and engine bytes were unchanged; explicit SQLite mode=ro adaptation changes access policy only.
One final generator and one final resolver were run for this segment. Unchanged successful native/source evidence was reused during packaging; no final native rerun for report formatting.
Fresh independent skeptic and root acceptance remain separate requirements. No production suite, repository landing, MAIN/device integration, human approval, source correction or hgm_gloss change is claimed.

The intervening yielding verb byin, written with its source apostrophe, owns the later ripens forth. The two pas clauses own in that and for respectively; final phyir and the recast first ngo bo are omitted as distributed, not nulled. ni has no separate exponent. The bracketed verse reference is apparatus. GMR English stays verbatim; no hgm_gloss is changed."
    }
  ]
}

===== english/317/report.md =====
# C05:317 — independent English-first proposal
Producer: Codex. All alignments remain PROVISIONAL; no hgm_gloss changes or semantic approval.
Read the full original source and C05:310–324 context. Original spine fields, full material corpus/master objects, physical bytes and historical governing inputs are retained under ../originals and ../evidence.
Challenge the source nominal rnam smin/result versus the other rnam smin/ripens assignment first; their argument roles require the recorded crossing English order. The two pas particles own distinct in that and for causal clauses.
Every retained span, selected occurrence, member parent, and every unwrapped source/English range is recorded in decisions.json. Uncertain/distributed correspondences are omitted with reasons, not converted to nulls.

      "note": "PROVISIONAL machine alignment by Codex, independent reconciliation. GMR wording and source Wylie remain verbatim; nothing is promoted to hgm_gloss. The four black occurrences preserve intrinsic and resultant blackness first in the definition and then in its explanation. The first shortened rnam smin owns the final noun result; the second owns the middle ripens. The intervening &apos;byin owns the final ripens forth as a local yielding-verb rendering, not a new master gloss. The two pas particles own in that and for at their respective causal clauses. ji lta bu owns how; whether Just belongs to the opening discourse marker or intensifies the interrogative is unresolved, so Just and &apos;o na remain unbanked. The question about ngo bo is recast as do we describe, not a lexical nature/describe pair. ni has no separate English word exponent. Final phyir and other distributed relations remain omitted, not nulled; four types of and the bracketed citation are retained as contextual English and apparatus without invented Tibetan links."
    }
  ]
}

===== reconciled/317/report.md =====
C05:317 — independent Codex reconciliation; every alignment remains PROVISIONAL.
Just has unresolved ownership between the opening discourse marker and the interrogative, so it remains unbanked while how is retained. Keep each of four black occurrences, three ripening/yielding positions, and two causal pas positions distinct. de dag and rang gi are lexical d5 units, and the positive virtue member is preserved.

===== english/318/report.md =====
# C05:318 — independent English-first proposal
Producer: Codex. All alignments remain PROVISIONAL; no hgm_gloss changes or semantic approval.
Read the full original source and C05:310–324 context. Original spine fields, full material corpus/master objects, physical bytes and historical governing inputs are retained under ../originals and ../evidence.
Challenge the negative mixture and causal scopes first. First gcig/in question remains uncertain and omitted; second gcig owns the explicit one. The three with exponents belong to first dang, second la, and second dang respectively.
Every retained span, selected occurrence, member parent, and every unwrapped source/English range is recorded in decisions.json. Uncertain/distributed correspondences are omitted with reasons, not converted to nulls.
Full five-ground screens are in five-ground-errata-review.json; errata.json is empty. Corpus equality proves only the compared text; no independent physical publication was inspected.
Canonical generator: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=5034 bytes. The actual complete stdout is body.html and native/generate-v1/stdout.bin; command, stdin, execution time and exit are preserved beside it.
Canonical resolver: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; all intended source and English ranges match. Complete native output is native/resolve-v1/stdout.bin. Successful generation was run once for this unchanged spec.
Measured: 30 spans; 0 nulls; 19 non-null d5 word pairs; 19 total d5; 4 d7 members.
Spec SHA256 38b716fbc91425389fc0a4f5c9bdcdd98278e67607f8dac569e4e72ad4e54d3a; body SHA256 0eb7ab9e40049594a491b5958210825e3474d92900aa592af5dbb29386c1996f.
Limits: no publication repair, repository acceptance, device integration, or production fidelity suite was performed by this original-proposal role. Independent reconciliation and fresh review are still separate requirements.

===== reconciled/318/report.md =====
C05:318 — independent Codex reconciliation; every alignment remains PROVISIONAL.
First gcig/in question and su/by are left uncertain and unbanked. Retain both negative mixture compounds and their members, exact mental-stream occurrences, and three with exponents. Refute the proposed BA-to-PA erratum as unresolved after the exact HGM BA headword counterevidence; preserve source spelling.
31 spans; 1 null; 20 non-null d5 word pairs; 20 total d5; 4 d7 members; 0 retained errata.
The topic null asserts only no separately alignable English word; subject-predicate syntax preserves its function. Uncertainty and distributed translation are omitted with reasons.
All 62 original proposals for this segment have exact identity/range/parent-aware dispositions in decisions.json; all final tuples and complete unwrapped ranges are recorded there.
Five refutation grounds for each suspicion are in five-ground-errata-review.json, with full original source, physical, corpus, master and registered-class evidence preserved through ../original-to-copy-manifest.json.
Original source/governance origin is 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309. No later through315 acceptance baseline was consumed.
Original generators: both angle replays EXIT=0, empty stderr, exact saved bodies; original resolver replays EXIT=0. All historical/superseded originals remain preserved.
Final generator EXIT=0; stdout=body.html, 5366 bytes; stderr=0 bytes. Actual native/generate-v1 contains stdin, stdout, stderr, exit and execution metadata.
Final resolver EXIT=0; all intended ranges match; rendered Tibetan and English exactly reproduce original source fields after removing generated null markers.
Spec 5425 bytes, SHA256 33b56037d0c0e9ea1bb5b182abd2ca5ac0dc2aeffed2883c60030e387d00f604; body SHA256 7bcbb1664d1f93c8a2a1ecd9fa521b8b893ebfe845e0cacbd6c8cf9ee1b5da66.
No supplied-head allowance is requested. Canonical generator, historical builder and engine bytes were unchanged; explicit SQLite mode=ro adaptation changes access policy only.
One final generator and one final resolver were run for this segment. Unchanged successful native/source evidence was reused during packaging; no final native rerun for report formatting.
Fresh independent skeptic and root acceptance remain separate requirements. No production suite, repository landing, MAIN/device integration, human approval, source correction or hgm_gloss change is claimed.
